    <td colspan="8"><p><strong>IEC 60601-1Medical electrical equipment—Part 1:General Requirements for basic safety and essential performance</strong>医用电气设备<strong> </strong>第<strong>1</strong>部分：基本安全和基本性能通用要求</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2</p></td>
    <td><p>Marking on the outside of ME EQUIPMENT or ME EQUIPMENT parts/ME设备和ME设系上的标记（见表C.1）</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2.1</p></td>
    <td><p>Minimum requirements for marking on ME EQUIPMENT and on interchangeable<br>parts/在ME设备和可更换部件上标记的最低要求</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the size of the ME EQUIPMENT, an ME EQUIPMENT part or an ACCESSORY, or the nature of its<br>ENCLOSURE, does not allow affixation of all markings specified in 7.2.2 to 7.2.20 (inclusive),then at least the markings as indicated in 7.2.2, 7.2.5, 7.2.6 (not for PERMANENTLY INSTALLED ME EQUIPMENT), 7.2.10 and 7.2.13 (if applicable) shall be affixed and the remaining markings shall be recorded in full in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS. /如果ME设备、ME设备部件或附件的尺寸或外壳性质不容许附加7.2.2～7.2.20所规定的所有标记时，至少应标记7.2.2、7.2.5、7.2.6（永久性安装的设备除外）、7.2.10和7.2.13（如适用）等所规定的标记。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查外部标识</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>见7.2.2、7.2.5、7.2.6、7.2.10回复</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>the remaining markings shall be recorded in full in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS./其余的标记应在随机文件中完整地记载。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Sign &amp;Symbols<br></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2.2</p></td>
    <td><p>Identification/标识</p></td>

    <td><p>ME EQUIPMENT shall be marked with:<br>ME 设备应标识以下信息：</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>– the name or trademark and contact information of the MANUFACTURER;<br>—制造商的名称或商标，制造商的联系方式；</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>– a MODEL OR TYPE REFERENCE;<br>– 型号或型式标记；</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>– a serial number or lot or batch identifier; and<br>– 序列号或批号或批次标识；和</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查铭牌</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>SN中包含批次信息</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>– the date of manufacture or use by date, if applicable.<br>– 如果适用，生产日期或使用截止日期。<br>The serial number, lot or batch identifier, and the date of manufacture may be provided in a<br>human readable code or through automatic identification technology such as barcodes or<br>RFID.序列号，批号或批次标识，生产日期可通过人可读代码或通过自动识别技术如条形码或电子标签的方式提供。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查铭牌</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Detachable components of the ME EQUIPMENT shall be marked with:<br>– the name or trademark of the MANUFACTURER; and<br>– a MODEL OR TYPE REFERENCE;<br>unless misidentification does not result in an unacceptable RISK.除非错误的识别不会带来不可接受的风险，ME设备的可分离元器件上应标识：<br>–制造商的名称或商标；和—型号或型式标记；</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>无此类设备</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Software that forms part of a PEMS shall be identified with a unique identifier, such as revision<br>level or date of release/issue. The identification shall be available to designated persons, e.g.<br>SERVICE PERSONNEL. The identification does not need to be on the outside of the<br>ME EQUIPMENT./构成医用可编程电气系统(PEMS)一部分的的软件应有唯一的识别标识，例如版本号或发布日期。该识别标识对于指定人员如维护人员可以利用的。该标识不需要标在设备外部。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>设备上无需软件版本号，服务人员可进入测试态查到此信息</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>服务人员可进入测试态查到此信息</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2.3</p></td>
    <td><p>Consult ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS/查阅随机文件</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>When appropriate, symbol ISO 7000-1641 (DB:2004-01) (see Table D.1, symbol 11) may be<br>used to advise the OPERATOR to consult the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS./如果适用，ISO 7000-1641规定的符号（见表D.1，符号11），可以用于建议操作者查阅随机文件。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>此条不是强制性要求</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>When consulting the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS is a mandatory action, safety sign IEC 60878 Safety 01 (see Table D.2, safety sign 10) shall be used instead of symbol ISO 7000-1641./当查阅随机文件是强制性要求时，则应采用IEC 60870安全标志（见表D.2，安全标志10）来代替ISO 7000-1641规定的符号。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查铭牌是否有小人翻书标志</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>机器上有此标识</p></td>
    <td><p>小人翻书为强制性要求，故适用，小人翻书标识必须是蓝底白图的<br><img src="images/cd76c31e0a3947476514bfa782bd0b0b0b6c8685df4a225b4d7964cbed3f6ba8.jpg"/></p></td>
  </tr>

  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2.5</p></td>
    <td><p>预期从其他设备获得供电的ME设备</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If ME EQUIPMENT is intended to receive its power from other electrical equipment in an ME SYSTEM and compliance with the requirements of this standard is dependent on that other equipment, one of the following shall be provided:如果ME设备预期从ME系统中其它设备获得供电，并且对本标准要求的符合取决于此“其它设备”，应提供以下要求中的一种：
– the name or trademark of the manufacturer of the other electrical equipment with a MODEL OR TYPE REFERENCE of the specified other equipment adjacent to the relevant connection point;–相关连接点附近，应注明供电设备制造商的名称或商标，供电设备的的型号或型式标记；
– placing safety sign ISO 7010-M002 (see Table D.2, safety sign 10) adjacent to the relevant connection point and listing of the required details in the instructions for use; or–在相关连接点附近放置安全符号ISO 7010-M002 （见表D.2，符号10）并列明使用说明中所需的细节。或
– using a special connector style that is not commonly available on the market and listing of the required details in the instructions for use.–使用市场上不广泛通用的专用连接器并列明使用说明中所需的细节。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>无此类设备</p></td>
  </tr>

    <td><p>7.2.6</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>除了永久安装的ME设备之外，这些标记应贴在包括网电源连接器在内的部件外面，更适宜在邻近连接点处。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>对于永久安装的ME设备，可连接的标称电源电压或电压范围应该标记在设备的内部或外部，更适宜贴在邻近电源接线端子处。</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>— 设备可连接的额定供电电压或额定电压范围。额定供电电压范围应在最小与最大电压值之间用连字符（-）连接。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>— 电源的类型，如相数（单相电源除外）和电流的类型；符号可采用IEC 60417-5032、5032-1、5032-2、5031、和5033 （DB:2002-10）（见表D.1 符号1、2、3、4和5）；</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>— 对于ⅡME设备，符号采用IEC60417-5172（DB:2003-02）（见表D.1符号9）。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p>7.2.7</p></td>
    <td><p>Electrical input power from the SUPPLY MAINS从供电网输入的功率</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The RATED input from the SUPPLY MAINS shall be marked on the ME EQUIPMENT. The RATED input shall be given in:
– amperes or volt-amperes, or
– if the power factor exceeds 0,9, in amperes, volt-amperes or watts.ME设备上应标识来自网电源的额定输入，额定输入应以如下方式表示：
–安培或伏安，或
–如果功率因数大于0.9，用安培，伏安或瓦特表示。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In the case of ME EQUIPMENT for one or several RATED voltage ranges, the RATED input shall always be given for the upper and lower limits of the range or ranges, if the range(s) is/are greater than ± 10 % of the mean value of the given range.当ME设备有一个或几个额定电压范围，若这（些）范围超过给定范围平均值±10%时，应标明这（些）范围输入功率的上、下限。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In the case of range limits which do not differ by more than 10 % from the mean value,marking of the input at the mean value of the range is sufficient.若电压范围的极限未超过其平均值的10%，则只需标明平均值输入功率。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the rating of ME EQUIPMENT includes both long-time and momentary current or volt-ampere
ratings, the marking shall include both long-time and the most relevant momentary voltampere
ratings, each plainly identified and indicated in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS.若ME设备额定值同时包括了长期的和瞬时的电流或伏安值，标记应同时包括长期和最相关的瞬时伏安额定值，并在随机文件中清楚地分别予以表明。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The marked input of ME EQUIPMENT provided with means for the connection of supply<br>conductors of other electrical equipment shall include the RATED (and marked) output of such<br>means.若ME设备附有供其他设备的电源连接装置，则ME设备所标的输入功率应包括对这些装置的额定（并标明）输出在内。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>ME EQUIPMENT or its parts shall be marked with a symbol, using the letters IP followed by the
designations described in IEC 60529, according to the classification in 6.3 (see Table D.3,Code 2).ME设备或其部件应依据6.3所述的分类（见表D.3 代码2）用字母IP后接上IEC60529中描述的符号来标记。
IPX0或IP0X类ME设备不需要这样标记。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>This requirement does not apply to parts that have been identified according to 4.6.<br>The degree of protection against electric shock as classified in 6.2 for all APPLIED PARTS shall be marked with the relevant symbol, i.e.,/本要求不适用于依据4.6已被识别的部件。<br>如6.2所述的电击防护程度分类，所有应用部分均应使用相关符号做标记。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For DEFIBRILLATION-PROOF APPLIED PARTS, symbols IEC 60417-5841, IEC 60417-5334, or<br>IEC 60417-5336 shall be used as applicable (all DB:2002-10) (see Table D.1, symbols 25, 26<br>and 27)./对防除颤应用部分，适用于IEC 60417-5841、IEC 60417-5334或IEC60417-5336规定的符号（见表D.1符号25、26和27）。（DB:2002-10全部）</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The relevant symbol shall be marked adjacent to or on the connector for the APPLIED PART,<br>unless either:<br>– there is no such connector, in which case the marking shall be on the APPLIED PART; or<br>– the connector is used for more than one APPLIED PART and the different APPLIED PARTS<br>have different classifications, in which case each APPLIED PART shall be marked with the<br>relevant symbol./相应符号应标记在应用部分连接器上或其邻近处，除非：<br>— 无该类连接器，此时应标记在应用部分上；或<br>— 如果连接器用于连接一个以上的应用部分，并且不同的应用部分具有不同的分类，在这种情况下，每个应用部分应使用相应的符号作标记。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the protection against the effect of the discharge of a cardiac defibrillator is partly in the PATIENT cable, safety sign ISO 7010-W001, shall be placed near the relevant outlet (see Table D.2, safety sign 2)../如果对心脏除颤器放电效应的防护部分在患者电缆中，ISO7010-W001的安全标记应安置在相应的插座附近（见表D.2，安全标记2）。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall explain that protection of the ME EQUIPMENT<br>against the effects of the discharge of a cardiac defibrillator is dependent upon the use of<br>appropriate cables./使用说明书应说明，ME设备对心脏除颤器放电效应的防护依赖于使用适当的电缆。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>If no marking is provided, ME EQUIPMENT is assumed to be suitable for CONTINUOUS OPERATION./如果没有标记，ME设备被认为适合连续运行。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For ME EQUIPMENT intended for non-CONTINUOUS OPERATION, the DUTY CYCLE shall be indicated<br>using an appropriate marking giving the maximum activation (on) time and the minimum<br>deactivation (off) time./对于预期非连续运行的ME设备，应采用适当的标记指示工作周期，给最大激活启动时间和最小激活关闭时间。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If special handling measures have to be taken during transport or storage, the packaging shall<br>be marked accordingly (see ISO 780)./若在运输或贮存中要采取特别的处理措施，在包装上应作出相应的标记 （见ISO 780）。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The type of battery and the mode of insertion (if applicable) shall be marked (see 15.4.3.2)./应标明电池的型号及其装入方法（如合适用）（见15.4.3.2）。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For batteries intended to be changed only by SERVICE PERSONNEL with the use of a TOOL, an<br>identifying marking referring to information stated in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS is<br>sufficient./预期仅由服务人员使用工具才能更换的电池，用一个引用随机文件中说明的识别标记即可。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Where lithium batteries or fuel cells are incorporated and where incorrect replacement would<br>result in an unacceptable RISK, a warning indicating that replacement by inadequately trained<br>personnel could result in a HAZARD (such as excessive temperatures, fire or explosion) shall<br>be given in addition to the identifying marking referring to information stated in the<br>ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS./在装有锂电池或燃料电池，并且由于不正确的更换可能导致不可接受的风险时，由培训不充分的人员更换可能导致危害（如过高的温度、火灾或爆炸）的警示性说明应附加到识别标记</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Numeric indications of parameters on ME EQUIPMENT shall be expressed in SI units according<br>to ISO 31 except the base quantities listed in Table 1 may be expressed in the indicated units,<br>which are outside the SI units system./ME设备参数的数值指示，应采用ISO 31的国际单位制表示，除了表1中列出的基本量可采用国际单位制以外的单位表示之外。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查温度计显示和随机文件中参数的单位是否为摄氏度</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>温度计符合要求<br>Resolution: 0.1℉/0.1℃<br>Measurement range:93.2℉-109.4℉(34.0℃-43.0℃)<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For application of SI units, their multiples and certain other units, ISO 1000 applies./对于国际单位制的应用，其倍数和某些其他单位，按ISO1000规定。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查温度计显示和随机文件中参数的单位是否为摄氏度</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>温度计符合要求<br>Resolution: 0.1℉/0.1℃<br>Measurement range:93.2℉-109.4℉(34.0℃-43.0℃)<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>7.5</p></td>
    <td><p>Safety signs/安全标识</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For the purpose of this clause, markings used to convey a warning, prohibition or mandatory<br>action that mitigates a RISK that is not obvious to the OPERATOR shall be a safety sign selected from ISO 7010.If a safety sign with an established meaning is appropriately used, the use of the general warning sign ISO 7010:2003-W001 (see Table D.2, safety sign 2) is not required./为达到本章目的，用于传递警告、禁止或强制性行动的标记应选择ISO 7010里的符号.规定的安全标识，以降低操作者不易察觉的风险。如果具有明确意义的安全符号被适当使用，那么一般性安全符号ISO7010:2003的使用不是必须的。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p><img src="images/573848db11d2bc6d5d9e7d268cf0b994d9cd16a36079d811b301e73ad637ca39.jpg"/></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If there is insufficient space to place the affirmative statement together with the safety sign on<br>the ME EQUIPMENT, it may be placed in the instructions for use./如果在ME设备上没有足够的空间将肯定性陈述和安全标识安置在一起，可将其放在使用说明书中。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查铭牌及说明书</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Sign &amp;Symbols<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The colours for safety signs are specified in ISO 3864-1 and it is important to use the specified colour/安全标识的颜色由ISO 3864-1规定，使用规定的颜色是重要的。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>建议此条不适用，因为1.彩色印刷成本高2.与风险分析暂时无关</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>A safety GENERAL SAFETY AND PRECAUTIONS should include the appropriate precautions or include instructions on how to reduce the<br>RISK/安全须知应包括恰当的预防措施或如何降低风险的说明。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书是否有警告标识及警告内容</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>列举几条有警告标识的内容</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Safety signs, including any supplementary symbol or text, shall be explained in the<br>instructions for use/安全标识、包括任何辅助符号或文本，均应在使用说明书中予以解释。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书是否有符号解释</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Sign &amp;Symbols<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>如果安全符号附近有补充文本，补充文本应采用预期操作者可接受的语言<br>。注4：在某些国家，需要不止一种语言。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>安全符号附近无文本标识</p></td>

    <td><p>ME EQUIPMENT shall be accompanied by documents containing at least the instructions for use<br>and a technical description. The ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS shall be regarded as a part of the<br>ME EQUIPMENT.The ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS shall identify the ME EQUIPMENT by including, as applicable, the following:/ME设备应附有至少包括使用说明书，技术说明书的文件。随机文件被认为是ME设备的一部分。如果适用，随机文件中应包括下述识别ME设备的信息：</p></td>

    <td><p>– name or trade-name of the MANUFACTURER and contact information to which the RESPONSIBLE<br>ORGANIZATION can refer;/—制造商名称或商标，可供责任组织查阅的制造商联系方式。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For CLASS I ME EQUIPMENT, the instructions for use shall include a warning statement to the<br>effect: “WARNING: To avoid the risk of electric shock, this equipment must only be connected<br>to a supply mains with protective earth.”/对于Ⅰ类ME设备，使用说明书应包括警告性陈述“警告：为了避免电击风险，本设备只应与带有保护接地的电网相连接”。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall provide the OPERATOR or RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION with<br>warnings regarding any significant RISKS of reciprocal interference posed by the presence of<br>the ME EQUIPMENT during specific investigations or treatments.使用说明书应该向操作者或责任组织提供有关ME设备在具体的检查或治疗期间，由相互干扰所引起的任何重大风险的警告。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall include information regarding potential electromagnetic or other<br>interference between the ME EQUIPMENT and other devices together with advice on ways to<br>avoid or minimize such interference.使用说明书应包括有关ME设备与其他器械之间潜在的电磁干扰或其他干扰的信息，并给出避免或减少该种干扰的建议措施。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p>OK</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the ME EQUIPMENT is provided with an integral MULTIPLE SOCKET-OUTLET, the instructions for use shall provide a warning statement that connecting electrical equipment to the MSO<br>effectively leads to creating an ME SYSTEM and the result can be a reduced level of safety. For the requirements that are applicable to an ME SYSTEM, the RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION shall be<br>referred to this standard.如果ME设备配备一个集成的多联插座，使用说明书应有警告性说明：将电气设备连接到多联插座，会有效地导致生成一个ME系统，其结果将会降低安全水平。本要求适用于任何ME系统，责任方应参考本标准。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

The instructions for use shall state this specification./如果ME设备预期与独立电源连接，那么该电源应被作为ME设备的一部分，或者该联合应被作为ME系统。</p></td>

    <td><p>For mains-operated ME EQUIPMENT with an additional power source not automatically<br>maintained in a fully usable condition, the instructions for use shall include a warning<br>statement referring to the necessity for periodic checking or replacement of such an additional<br>power source/对由电网供电并带有附加电源的ME设备，若其附加电源不能自动地保持在完全可用的状态，使用说明书应提出警告，规定应对该附加电源进行定期检查和更换。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If leakage from a battery would result in an unacceptable RISK, the instructions for use shallinclude a warning to remove the battery if the ME EQUIPMENT is not likely to be used for sometime./如果电池的漏液会导致不可接受的风险，使用说明书中应有警告性说明：若在一段时间内可能不使用ME设备时，应取出这些电池。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall include:<br>– a brief description of the ME EQUIPMENT;<br>– how the ME EQUIPMENT functions; and<br>– the significant physical and performance characteristics of the ME EQUIPMENT.<br>If applicable, this description shall include the expected positions of the OPERATOR, PATIENT and other persons near the ME EQUIPMENT in NORMAL USE (see 9.2.2.3)./使用说明书应包括：<br>— ME设备简要的描述；<br>— ME设备的功能；和<br>— ME设备重要的物理和性能参数。<br>如果适用，应包括ME设备在正常使用时操作者、患者和靠近ME设备的其他人员的预期位置的描述。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If an APPLIANCE COUPLER or MAINS PLUG or other separable plug is used as the isolation means to satisfy 8.11.1 a),the instructions for use shall contain an instruction not to position the ME EQUIPMENT so that it is difficult to operate the disconnection device./如果使用设备连接装置或网电源插头或可分离式插头来作为分断方法以满足8.11.1a)要求，则使用说明书中应包括以下说明：不要将ME设备安置在<br>难以操作断开装置的位置。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain the necessary information for the OPERATOR to bring the ME EQUIPMENT into operation including such items as any initial control settings, connection to or positioning of the PATIENT, etc.The instructions for use shall detail any treatment or handling needed before the ME EQUIPMENT, its parts, or ACCESSORIES can be used.使用说明书应包括操作者启动运转ME设备的必要的信息，包括诸如任何初始控制的设置、患者的连接和定位等事项。<br>使用说明书应详细说明，在ME设备、其部件或附件可被使用之前必需的处理或操作。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p> Instruction for use</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>7.9.2.9</p></td>
    <td><p>Operating instructions/操作说明</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain all information necessary to operate the ME EQUIPMENT in accordance with its specification. This shall include explanation of the functions of controls,displays and signals, the sequence of operation, and connection and disconnection of detachable parts and ACCESSORIES, and replacement of material that is consumed during operation./使用说明书应该包括对按照其技术条件运行ME设备的所有必要信息。应包括如控制器、显示器和信号的功能说明，操作顺序、可拆卸部件和附件的连接和拆卸方法以及使用过程中消耗材料的更换方法。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Instruction for use</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The meanings of figures, symbols, warning statements, abbreviations and indicator lights on ME EQUIPMENT shall be explained in the instructions for use./ME设备上的图形、符号、警告性声明，缩写和指示灯的含义，应在使用说明书中予以说明。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Signs and symbols</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall list all system messages, error messages and fault messages that are generated, unless these messages are self-explanatory.                               The list shall include an explanation of messages including important causes, and possible action(s) by the OPERATOR, if any, that are necessary to resolve the situation indicated by the message./使用说明书应列出生成的所有系统信息、错误信息和故障信息。除非这些信息是不言自明的。<br>所列举的信息清单应包括重要起因的解释，并且如有信息指示需要解决的情况，操作者可能的行动。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain the necessary information for the OPERATOR to safely terminate the operation of the ME EQUIPMENT./使用说明书应包含用于操作者安全终止ME设备运行的必要的信息。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For ME EQUIPMENT parts or ACCESSORIES that can become contaminated through contact with<br>the PATIENT or with body fluids or expired gases during NORMAL USE, the instructions for use<br>shall contain:<br>– details about cleaning and disinfection or sterilization methods that may be used; and<br>– list the applicable parameters such as temperature, pressure, humidity, time limits and<br>number of cycles that such ME EQUIPMENT parts or ACCESSORIES can tolerate./在正常使用期间与患者、体液或者呼气相接触的有可能被污染的ME设备部件或其附件，使用说明书应包括：<br>— 可使用的清洗、消毒或灭菌方法的详细说明；<br>— 列出该ME设备部件或其附件所能承受的适当的参数，如温度、压力、湿度、时间限制和运行循环次数。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>This requirement does not apply to any material, component, ACCESSORY or ME EQUIPMENT that is marked as intended for a single use unless the MANUFACTURER specifies that the material, component, ACCESSORY or ME EQUIPMENT is to be cleaned, disinfected or sterilized before use/该要求不适用于标记有“一次性使用”的任何材料、元件、附件或ME设备，除非制造商规定在使用这些材料、元件、附件或ME设备之前应进行清洗、消毒和灭菌</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p>MAINTENANCE/维护</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall instruct the OPERATOR or RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION in sufficient detail concerning preventive inspection, MAINTENANCE and calibration to be performed by them, including the frequency of such MAINTENANCE./使用说明书必须向操作者或服务人员详细说明由他们自己来进行的预防性检查保养和校验，以及保养的周期。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书有无使用者自己预防性检查保养和校验，以及保养的周期。</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall provide information for the safe performance of such routine MAINTENANCE necessary to ensure the continued safe use of the ME EQUIPMENT./这类说明书必须提供安全地执行常规保养的资料以确保ME设备的继续安全使用。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Additionally, the instructions for use shall identify the parts on which preventive inspection
and MAINTENANCE shall be performed by SERVICE PERSONNEL, including the periods to be
applied, but not necessarily including details about the actual performance of such
MAINTENANCE./此外，使用说明书还必须提出哪些部件必须由服务人员进行预防性检查和保养，以及适用的周期，但不必包括执行这种保养的具体细节。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>

anyone other than SERVICE PERSONNEL, the instructions for use shall contain instructions to ensure adequate MAINTENANCE./对于配有预期由非服务人员维护的可充电电池的ME设备，使用说明书应包含相关说明，以确保其充分的维护。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If ME EQUIPMENT is intended to receive its power from other equipment in an ME SYSTEM, the instructions for use shall sufficiently specify such other equipment to ensure compliance with the requirements of this standard (e.g. part number, RATED voltage, maximum or minimum power, protection class, intermittent or continuous service)./如果ME设备预期从一个ME系统中的其他设备获得电源，使用说明书应对该类设备做出充分的说明，以确保符合本标准的要求（如部件编号、额定电压、最大或最小功率、保护分类、间歇或连续服务等）。备注：在本标准第一版和第二版中所指的“指定电源”可认为是相同ME设备的另一部分或一<br>个ME系统中的其他设备。同理，一个电池充电器可认为是ME设备的一个部分或ME系统中的其<br>他设备。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>– the date of manufacture or use by date, if applicable.<br>– 如果适用，生产日期或使用截止日期。<br>The serial number, lot or batch identifier, and the date of manufacture may be provided in a<br>human readable code or through automatic identification technology such as barcodes or<br>RFID.序列号，批号或批次标识，生产日期可通过人可读代码或通过自动识别技术如条形码或电子标签的方式提供。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>无此类设备</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Where no marking of the ACCESSORIES is practicable,<br>these markings may be affixed to the individual packaging./如果附件不宜作标识，标记应贴在单包装上。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If ME EQUIPMENT is intended to receive its power from other electrical equipment in an ME SYSTEM and compliance with the requirements of this standard is dependent on that other equipment, one of the following shall be provided:如果ME设备预期从ME系统中其它设备获得供电，并且对本标准要求的符合取决于此“其它设备”，应提供以下要求中的一种：
– the name or trademark of the manufacturer of the other electrical equipment with a MODEL OR TYPE REFERENCE of the specified other equipment adjacent to the relevant connection point;–相关连接点附近，应注明供电设备制造商的名称或商标，供电设备的的型号或型式标记；
– placing safety sign ISO 7010-M002 (see Table D.2, safety sign 10) adjacent to the relevant connection point and listing of the required details in the instructions for use; or–在相关连接点附近放置安全符号ISO 7010-M002 （见表D.2，符号10）并列明使用说明中所需的细节。或
– using a special connector style that is not commonly available on the market and listing of the required details in the instructions for use.–使用市场上不广泛通用的专用连接器并列明使用说明中所需的细节。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>除了永久安装的ME设备之外，这些标记应贴在包括网电源连接器在内的部件外面，更适宜在邻近连接点处。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>对于永久安装的ME设备，可连接的标称电源电压或电压范围应该标记在设备的内部或外部，更适宜贴在邻近电源接线端子处。</p></td>

    <td><p>— 设备可连接的额定供电电压或额定电压范围。额定供电电压范围应在最小与最大电压值之间用连字符（-）连接。</p></td>

    <td><p>Electrical input power from the SUPPLY MAINS从供电网输入的功率</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The RATED input from the SUPPLY MAINS shall be marked on the ME EQUIPMENT. The RATED input shall be given in:
– amperes or volt-amperes, or
– if the power factor exceeds 0,9, in amperes, volt-amperes or watts.ME设备上应标识来自网电源的额定输入，额定输入应以如下方式表示：
–安培或伏安，或
–如果功率因数大于0.9，用安培，伏安或瓦特表示。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In the case of ME EQUIPMENT for one or several RATED voltage ranges, the RATED input shall always be given for the upper and lower limits of the range or ranges, if the range(s) is/are greater than ± 10 % of the mean value of the given range.当ME设备有一个或几个额定电压范围，若这（些）范围超过给定范围平均值±10%时，应标明这（些）范围输入功率的上、下限。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In the case of range limits which do not differ by more than 10 % from the mean value,marking of the input at the mean value of the range is sufficient.若电压范围的极限未超过其平均值的10%，则只需标明平均值输入功率。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the rating of ME EQUIPMENT includes both long-time and momentary current or volt-ampere
ratings, the marking shall include both long-time and the most relevant momentary voltampere
ratings, each plainly identified and indicated in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS.若ME设备额定值同时包括了长期的和瞬时的电流或伏安值，标记应同时包括长期和最相关的瞬时伏安额定值，并在随机文件中清楚地分别予以表明。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The marked input of ME EQUIPMENT provided with means for the connection of supply<br>conductors of other electrical equipment shall include the RATED (and marked) output of such<br>means.若ME设备附有供其他设备的电源连接装置，则ME设备所标的输入功率应包括对这些装置的额定（并标明）输出在内。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>ME EQUIPMENT or its parts shall be marked with a symbol, using the letters IP followed by the
designations described in IEC 60529, according to the classification in 6.3 (see Table D.3,Code 2).ME设备或其部件应依据6.3所述的分类（见表D.3 代码2）用字母IP后接上IEC60529中描述的符号来标记。
IPX0或IP0X类ME设备不需要这样标记。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For DEFIBRILLATION-PROOF APPLIED PARTS, symbols IEC 60417-5841, IEC 60417-5334, or<br>IEC 60417-5336 shall be used as applicable (all DB:2002-10) (see Table D.1, symbols 25, 26<br>and 27)./对防除颤应用部分，适用于IEC 60417-5841、IEC 60417-5334或IEC60417-5336规定的符号（见表D.1符号25、26和27）。（DB:2002-10全部）</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The relevant symbol shall be marked adjacent to or on the connector for the APPLIED PART,<br>unless either:<br>– there is no such connector, in which case the marking shall be on the APPLIED PART; or<br>– the connector is used for more than one APPLIED PART and the different APPLIED PARTS<br>have different classifications, in which case each APPLIED PART shall be marked with the<br>relevant symbol./相应符号应标记在应用部分连接器上或其邻近处，除非：<br>— 无该类连接器，此时应标记在应用部分上；或<br>— 如果连接器用于连接一个以上的应用部分，并且不同的应用部分具有不同的分类，在这种情况下，每个应用部分应使用相应的符号作标记。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the protection against the effect of the discharge of a cardiac defibrillator is partly in the PATIENT cable, safety sign ISO 7010-W001, shall be placed near the relevant outlet (see Table D.2, safety sign 2)../如果对心脏除颤器放电效应的防护部分在患者电缆中，ISO7010-W001的安全标记应安置在相应的插座附近（见表D.2，安全标记2）。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall explain that protection of the ME EQUIPMENT<br>against the effects of the discharge of a cardiac defibrillator is dependent upon the use of<br>appropriate cables./使用说明书应说明，ME设备对心脏除颤器放电效应的防护依赖于使用适当的电缆。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>For ME EQUIPMENT intended for non-CONTINUOUS OPERATION, the DUTY CYCLE shall be indicated<br>using an appropriate marking giving the maximum activation (on) time and the minimum<br>deactivation (off) time./对于预期非连续运行的ME设备，应采用适当的标记指示工作周期，给最大激活启动时间和最小激活关闭时间。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For batteries intended to be changed only by SERVICE PERSONNEL with the use of a TOOL, an<br>identifying marking referring to information stated in the ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS is<br>sufficient./预期仅由服务人员使用工具才能更换的电池，用一个引用随机文件中说明的识别标记即可。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Where lithium batteries or fuel cells are incorporated and where incorrect replacement would<br>result in an unacceptable RISK, a warning indicating that replacement by inadequately trained<br>personnel could result in a HAZARD (such as excessive temperatures, fire or explosion) shall<br>be given in addition to the identifying marking referring to information stated in the<br>ACCOMPANYING DOCUMENTS./在装有锂电池或燃料电池，并且由于不正确的更换可能导致不可接受的风险时，由培训不充分的人员更换可能导致危害（如过高的温度、火灾或爆炸）的警示性说明应附加到识别标记</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Numeric indications of parameters on ME EQUIPMENT shall be expressed in SI units according<br>to ISO 31 except the base quantities listed in Table 1 may be expressed in the indicated units,<br>which are outside the SI units system./ME设备参数的数值指示，应采用ISO 31的国际单位制表示，除了表1中列出的基本量可采用国际单位制以外的单位表示之外。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查温度计显示和随机文件中参数的单位是否为摄氏度</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>温度计符合要求<br>Specification<br>Resolution: 0.1℉/0.1℃<br>Measurement range:93.2℉-109.4℉(34.0℃-43.0℃)<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For application of SI units, their multiples and certain other units, ISO 1000 applies./对于国际单位制的应用，其倍数和某些其他单位，按ISO1000规定。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查温度计显示和随机文件中参数的单位是否为摄氏度</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>温度计符合要求<br>Specification<br>Resolution: 0.1℉/0.1℃<br>Measurement range:93.2℉-109.4℉(34.0℃-43.0℃)<br></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>7.5</p></td>
    <td><p>Safety signs/安全标识</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For the purpose of this clause, markings used to convey a warning, prohibition or mandatory<br>action that mitigates a RISK that is not obvious to the OPERATOR shall be a safety sign selected from ISO 7010.If a safety sign with an established meaning is appropriately used, the use of the general warning sign ISO 7010:2003-W001 (see Table D.2, safety sign 2) is not required./为达到本章目的，用于传递警告、禁止或强制性行动的标记应选择ISO 7010里的符号.规定的安全标识，以降低操作者不易察觉的风险。如果具有明确意义的安全符号被适当使用，那么一般性安全符号ISO7010:2003的使用不是必须的。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p><img src="images/aac6a1805e17d6d0668b5b5201edfdb5dd0c212fab56000bf13de156a23a0fc0.jpg"/></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If there is insufficient space to place the affirmative statement together with the safety sign on<br>the ME EQUIPMENT, it may be placed in the instructions for use./如果在ME设备上没有足够的空间将肯定性陈述和安全标识安置在一起，可将其放在使用说明书中。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查铭牌及说明书</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>signs and symbols</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The colours for safety signs are specified in ISO 3864-1 and it is important to use the specified colour/安全标识的颜色由ISO 3864-1规定，使用规定的颜色是重要的。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>建议此条不适用，因为1.彩色印刷成本高2.与风险分析暂时无关</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>A safety GENERAL SAFETY AND PRECAUTIONS should include the appropriate precautions or include instructions on how to reduce the<br>RISK/安全须知应包括恰当的预防措施或如何降低风险的说明。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书是否有警告标识及警告内容</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>列举几条有警告标识的内容</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Safety signs, including any supplementary symbol or text, shall be explained in the<br>instructions for use/安全标识、包括任何辅助符号或文本，均应在使用说明书中予以解释。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书是否有符号解释</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>signs and symbols</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>如果安全符号附近有补充文本，补充文本应采用预期操作者可接受的语言<br>。注4：在某些国家，需要不止一种语言。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>安全符号附近无文本标识</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For CLASS I ME EQUIPMENT, the instructions for use shall include a warning statement to the<br>effect: “WARNING: To avoid the risk of electric shock, this equipment must only be connected<br>to a supply mains with protective earth.”/对于Ⅰ类ME设备，使用说明书应包括警告性陈述“警告：为了避免电击风险，本设备只应与带有保护接地的电网相连接”。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall provide the OPERATOR or RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION with<br>warnings regarding any significant RISKS of reciprocal interference posed by the presence of<br>the ME EQUIPMENT during specific investigations or treatments.使用说明书应该向操作者或责任组织提供有关ME设备在具体的检查或治疗期间，由相互干扰所引起的任何重大风险的警告。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p>OK</p></td>

    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information<br></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall include information regarding potential electromagnetic or other<br>interference between the ME EQUIPMENT and other devices together with advice on ways to<br>avoid or minimize such interference.使用说明书应包括有关ME设备与其他器械之间潜在的电磁干扰或其他干扰的信息，并给出避免或减少该种干扰的建议措施。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书中有无电磁兼容方面内容</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information<br></p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If the ME EQUIPMENT is provided with an integral MULTIPLE SOCKET-OUTLET, the instructions for use shall provide a warning statement that connecting electrical equipment to the MSO<br>effectively leads to creating an ME SYSTEM and the result can be a reduced level of safety. For the requirements that are applicable to an ME SYSTEM, the RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION shall be<br>referred to this standard.如果ME设备配备一个集成的多联插座，使用说明书应有警告性说明：将电气设备连接到多联插座，会有效地导致生成一个ME系统，其结果将会降低安全水平。本要求适用于任何ME系统，责任方应参考本标准。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

The instructions for use shall state this specification./如果ME设备预期与独立电源连接，那么该电源应被作为ME设备的一部分，或者该联合应被作为ME系统。</p></td>

    <td><p>For mains-operated ME EQUIPMENT with an additional power source not automatically<br>maintained in a fully usable condition, the instructions for use shall include a warning<br>statement referring to the necessity for periodic checking or replacement of such an additional<br>power source/对由电网供电并带有附加电源的ME设备，若其附加电源不能自动地保持在完全可用的状态，使用说明书应提出警告，规定应对该附加电源进行定期检查和更换。</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If leakage from a battery would result in an unacceptable RISK, the instructions for use shallinclude a warning to remove the battery if the ME EQUIPMENT is not likely to be used for sometime./如果电池的漏液会导致不可接受的风险，使用说明书中应有警告性说明：若在一段时间内可能不使用ME设备时，应取出这些电池。</p></td>

    <td><p>If installation of the ME EQUIPMENT or its parts is required, the instructions for use shall<br>contain:<br>– a reference to where the installation instructions are to be found (e.g. the technical<br>description), or<br>– contact information for persons designated by the MANUFACTURER as qualified to perform<br>the installation./如果需要对ME设备或其部件进行安装，使用说明书应包括：<br>— 可找到安装说明的参考资料（例如技术说明书）；或<br>— 与制造商指定的有资格执行安装的人员进行联系的信息。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If an APPLIANCE COUPLER or MAINS PLUG or other separable plug is used as the isolation means to satisfy 8.11.1 a),the instructions for use shall contain an instruction not to position the ME EQUIPMENT so that it is difficult to operate the disconnection device./如果使用设备连接装置或网电源插头或可分离式插头来作为分断方法以满足8.11.1a)要求，则使用说明书中应包括以下说明：不要将ME设备安置在<br>难以操作断开装置的位置。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain the necessary information for the OPERATOR to bring the ME EQUIPMENT into operation including such items as any initial control settings, connection to or positioning of the PATIENT, etc.The instructions for use shall detail any treatment or handling needed before the ME EQUIPMENT, its parts, or ACCESSORIES can be used.使用说明书应包括操作者启动运转ME设备的必要的信息，包括诸如任何初始控制的设置、患者的连接和定位等事项。<br>使用说明书应详细说明，在ME设备、其部件或附件可被使用之前必需的处理或操作。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p> Instruction for use</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>7.9.2.9</p></td>
    <td><p>Operating instructions/操作说明</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain all information necessary to operate the ME EQUIPMENT in accordance with its specification. This shall include explanation of the functions of controls,displays and signals, the sequence of operation, and connection and disconnection of detachable parts and ACCESSORIES, and replacement of material that is consumed during operation./使用说明书应该包括对按照其技术条件运行ME设备的所有必要信息。应包括如控制器、显示器和信号的功能说明，操作顺序、可拆卸部件和附件的连接和拆卸方法以及使用过程中消耗材料的更换方法。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Instruction for use</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The meanings of figures, symbols, warning statements, abbreviations and indicator lights on ME EQUIPMENT shall be explained in the instructions for use./ME设备上的图形、符号、警告性声明，缩写和指示灯的含义，应在使用说明书中予以说明。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>CONTENTS AND DISPLAY INDICATORS
 signs and symbols</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall list all system messages, error messages and fault messages that are generated, unless these messages are self-explanatory.                               The list shall include an explanation of messages including important causes, and possible action(s) by the OPERATOR, if any, that are necessary to resolve the situation indicated by the message./使用说明书应列出生成的所有系统信息、错误信息和故障信息。除非这些信息是不言自明的。<br>所列举的信息清单应包括重要起因的解释，并且如有信息指示需要解决的情况，操作者可能的行动。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

  </tr>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall contain the necessary information for the OPERATOR to safely terminate the operation of the ME EQUIPMENT./使用说明书应包含用于操作者安全终止ME设备运行的必要的信息。</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For ME EQUIPMENT parts or ACCESSORIES that can become contaminated through contact with<br>the PATIENT or with body fluids or expired gases during NORMAL USE, the instructions for use<br>shall contain:<br>– details about cleaning and disinfection or sterilization methods that may be used; and<br>– list the applicable parameters such as temperature, pressure, humidity, time limits and<br>number of cycles that such ME EQUIPMENT parts or ACCESSORIES can tolerate./在正常使用期间与患者、体液或者呼气相接触的有可能被污染的ME设备部件或其附件，使用说明书应包括：<br>— 可使用的清洗、消毒或灭菌方法的详细说明；<br>— 列出该ME设备部件或其附件所能承受的适当的参数，如温度、压力、湿度、时间限制和运行循环次数。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>This requirement does not apply to any material, component, ACCESSORY or ME EQUIPMENT that is marked as intended for a single use unless the MANUFACTURER specifies that the material, component, ACCESSORY or ME EQUIPMENT is to be cleaned, disinfected or sterilized before use/该要求不适用于标记有“一次性使用”的任何材料、元件、附件或ME设备，除非制造商规定在使用这些材料、元件、附件或ME设备之前应进行清洗、消毒和灭菌</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>

    <td><p>7.9.2.13</p></td>
    <td><p>MAINTENANCE/维护</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall instruct the OPERATOR or RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION in sufficient detail concerning preventive inspection, MAINTENANCE and calibration to be performed by them, including the frequency of such MAINTENANCE./使用说明书必须向操作者或服务人员详细说明由他们自己来进行的预防性检查保养和校验，以及保养的周期。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书有无使用者自己预防性检查保养和校验，以及保养的周期。</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall provide information for the safe performance of such routine MAINTENANCE necessary to ensure the continued safe use of the ME EQUIPMENT./这类说明书必须提供安全地执行常规保养的资料以确保ME设备的继续安全使用。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Additionally, the instructions for use shall identify the parts on which preventive inspection
and MAINTENANCE shall be performed by SERVICE PERSONNEL, including the periods to be
applied, but not necessarily including details about the actual performance of such
MAINTENANCE./此外，使用说明书还必须提出哪些部件必须由服务人员进行预防性检查和保养，以及适用的周期，但不必包括执行这种保养的具体细节。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Care and cleaning</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>For ME EQUIPMENT containing rechargeable batteries that are intended to be maintained by
anyone other than SERVICE PERSONNEL, the instructions for use shall contain instructions to ensure adequate MAINTENANCE./对于配有预期由非服务人员维护的可充电电池的ME设备，使用说明书应包含相关说明，以确保其充分的维护。</p></td>

    <td><p>The instructions for use shall include a list of ACCESSORIES, detachable parts and materials<br>that the MANUFACTURER has determined are intended for use with the ME EQUIPMENT./使用说明书中应包括制造商规定的、预期与ME设备一起使用的附件、可拆卸部件和材料的清单。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>If ME EQUIPMENT is intended to receive its power from other equipment in an ME SYSTEM, the instructions for use shall sufficiently specify such other equipment to ensure compliance with the requirements of this standard (e.g. part number, RATED voltage, maximum or minimum power, protection class, intermittent or continuous service)./如果ME设备预期从一个ME系统中的其他设备获得电源，使用说明书应对该类设备做出充分的说明，以确保符合本标准的要求（如部件编号、额定电压、最大或最小功率、保护分类、间歇或连续服务等）。备注：在本标准第一版和第二版中所指的“指定电源”可认为是相同ME设备的另一部分或一<br>个ME系统中的其他设备。同理，一个电池充电器可认为是ME设备的一个部分或ME系统中的其<br>他设备。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>The instructions for use shall provide advice on the proper disposal of waste products,<br>residues, etc. and of the ME EQUIPMENT and ACCESSORIES at the end of their EXPECTED SERVICE LIFE./使用说明应提供正确处理废弃物，残渣等以及在预期寿命末期的ME设备和<br>附件方面的建议。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.2 of the general standard, ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS<br>specified for use only in a shielded location SPECIAL ENVIRONMENT shall be labelled with a<br>CLEARLY LEGIBLE warning that they should be used only in the specified type of shielded<br>location.作为对IEC 60601-1 中7.2的补充，仅用于屏蔽场所的ME设备或ME系统，应清楚标识：只能用于指定类型的屏蔽场所。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.2 of the general standard, the instructions for use shall<br>include the following:作为对IEC 60601-1中7.9.2的补充，使用说明中应包含如下内容：</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>a statement of the environments for which the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM is suitable.<br>Relevant exclusions, as determined by RISK ANALYSIS, shall also be listed, e.g. hospitals<br>except for near active HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT and the RF shielded room of an ME SYSTEM<br>for magnetic resonance imaging, where the intensity of EM DISTURBANCES is high.a)对ME设备或ME系统适用的环境说明，应罗列通过风险分析确定的不能使用的环境，如：除了靠近有源高频手术设备的医院和用于核磁共振成像的具有高电磁干扰强度的RF屏蔽室。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p><strong>Notice</strong><br>13. Do not measure body temperature in an environment with strong EM interference (examples include places close to a working microwave, induction cooker, or cellphone in use) as EM interference may cause errors in the reading or even device failure.</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>b) * the performance of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM that was determined to be ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE and a description of what the OPERATOR can expect if the ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE is lost or degraded due to EM DISTURBANCES (the defined term “ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE” need not be used).ME设备或ME系统的基本性能以及，当由于电磁干扰使该关键性能缺失或下降时操作者可以进行的操作。（基本性能 “ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE”可以不体现在描述中）。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>c) * a warning statement to the effect that “WARNING: Use of this equipment adjacent to or<br>stacked with other equipment should be avoided because it could result in improper<br>operation. If such use is necessary, this equipment and the other equipment should be<br>observed to verify that they are operating normally.”<br>The MANUFACTURER of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM may provide a description or list of<br>equipment with which the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM has been tested in a stacked or<br>adjacent configuration and with which stacked or adjacent use resulted in normal<br>operation.c) 警告性声明：应避免此设备与其他设备连接,因为这可能导致设备的运行异常。如果这种使用是必须的，需要观察该设备及其他设备以确认他们是否能正常工作。ME设备或ME系统的制造商可以提供一份描述或设备清单，在此描述或清单中的设备已经完成与ME设备或ME系统的连接和测试且证明能正常工作。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p><strong>Notice</strong><br>13. Do not measure body temperature in an environment with strong EM interference (examples include places close to a working microwave, induction cooker, or cellphone in use) as EM interference may cause errors in the reading or even device failure.</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>d) * a list of all cables and maximum lengths of cables (if applicable), transducers and other<br>ACCESSORIES that are replaceable by the RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION and that are likely to<br>affect compliance of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM with the requirements of Clause 7<br>(EMISSIONS) and Clause 8 (IMMUNITY). ACCESSORIES may be specified either generically<br>(e.g. shielded cable, load impedance) or specifically (e.g. by MANUFACTURER and MODEL OR<br>TYPE REFERENCE).<br>Transducers and cables specified by the MANUFACTURER of the ME EQUIPMENT or<br>ME SYSTEM as replacement parts for internal components need not be listed.应提供一份清单，清单应包含：所有的线缆以及线缆的最大长度（如果适用）、转换器和其它可被责任机构更换的且可能会影响ME设备或ME系统对本标准条款7和条款8的符合性的附件。可采用概括性语言（如屏蔽导线，负载电阻）或描述性的语言（如制造商或型号）来说明附件。制造商规定的，作为ME设备或ME系统可更换部件的内部转换器或内部线缆不用罗列。</p></td>

    <td><p>f) * a warning statement to the effect that: “WARNING: Portable RF communications<br>equipment (including peripherals such as antenna cables and external antennas) should<br>be used no closer than 30 cm (12 inches) to any part of the [ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM],<br>including cables specified by the manufacturer. Otherwise, degradation of the<br>performance of this equipment could result.”<br>In the above warning, “[ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM]” shall be replaced with the MODEL OR<br>TYPE REFERENCE of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM.f) 警告性声明：可携带式射频通讯设备（包括包括外围设备，如天线、电缆和外接天线等）在使用时，应与ME设备或ME系统的任一部件（包括制造商要求的线缆）保持至少30cm(12 inches)的距离，否则可能会导致该设备性能下降。<br>在上述警告中“ME设备或ME系统”应被设备的型号替代。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p>5.2.1.2</p></td>
    <td><p>Requirements applicable to ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS classified class A<br>according to CISPR 11   对CISPR 11 A类ME设备和ME系统的要求</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.2 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT and<br>ME SYSTEMS that are classified class A according to CISPR 11, the instructions for use shall<br>include the following note:<br>NOTE The EMISSIONS characteristics of this equipment make it suitable for use in industrial areas and hospitals<br>(CISPR 11 class A). If it is used in a residential environment (for which CISPR 11 class B is normally required) this<br>equipment might not offer adequate protection to radio-frequency communication services. The user might need to<br>take mitigation measures, such as relocating or re-orienting the equipment.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.2 的补充，CISPR 11中A类ME设备和ME系统，其使用说明中应包含以下备注：<br>备注：该设备的发射特性使其适合于工业环境和医院使用(CISPR 11 class A)。 如果在居住环境中使用该设备（此类设备需要符合CISPR 11 class B的要求），该设备可能无法对射频通讯服务提供足够的保护。使用者可能需要采取补救措施，如设备的搬迁或重新定向。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>

  <tr>

    <td><p>Requirements applicable to all ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS对所有ME设备和ME系统的要求</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, the technical description shall<br>describe precautions to be taken to prevent adverse events to the PATIENT and OPERATOR due<br>to ELECTROMAGNETIC DISTURBANCES.For all ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS, the technical description shall include the following<br>information:作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，技术说明中应描述由电磁干扰引起对患者和操作者的不良事件的预防措施。对所有ME设备和ME系统，技术说明应包含如下信息：</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>a) the compliance for each EMISSIONS and IMMUNITY standard or test specified by this<br>collateral standard, e.g. EMISSIONS class and group and IMMUNITY TEST LEVEL;符合本并列标准要求的所有辐射及抗扰标准，如发射等级和组别、抗扰测试等级；</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>b) any deviations from this collateral standard and allowances used;与本并列标准和允差值的任何偏离；</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>c) * all necessary instructions for maintaining BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE with<br>regard to ELECTROMAGNETIC DISTURBANCES for the EXPECTED SERVICE LIFE.所有维持基本安全性能所需的与预期使用寿命内电磁干扰相关的介绍。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>5.2.2.2</p></td>
    <td><p>Requirements applicable to ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS specified for use<br>only in a shielded location SPECIAL ENVIRONMENT仅用于屏蔽场所的ME设备和系统</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT and<br>ME SYSTEMS specified for use only in a shielded location (see 7.1.5), the technical description<br>shall include the following information:作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，仅用于屏蔽场所的ME设备和ME系统（见7.1.5），其技术说明中应包含以下信息：</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>a) a warning to the effect that: “WARNING: Failure to use this equipment in the specified<br>type of shielded location could result in degradation of the performance of this equipment,<br>interference with other equipment or interference with radio services”;警告：在要求的屏蔽场所类型以外的环境中使用可能会导致设备性能下降、对其它设备或射频服务的干扰。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>b) specifications for the shielded location, including:<br>– minimum RF shielding effectiveness;<br>– for each cable that enters or exits the shielded location, the minimum RF filter<br>attenuation; and<br>– the frequency range(s) over which the specifications apply;对屏蔽场所的要求，包括：<br>– 最低的射频屏蔽能力；<br>– 对每个进出屏蔽场所线缆，最低的射频滤波衰减；和<br>– 以上要求的使用频率范围；</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>c) recommended test methods for measurement of RF shielding effectiveness and RF filter<br>attenuation;推荐的用于测量射频屏蔽能力的方法；</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>d) one or more of the following and a recommendation that a notice containing this<br>information be posted at the entrance(s) to the shielded location:<br>– a specification of the EMISSIONS characteristics of other equipment allowed inside the<br>shielded location with the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM;<br>– a list of specific equipment allowed;<br>– a list of types of equipment prohibited.以下要求中的一条或多条，以及建议：包含此信息的通知应粘贴在在屏蔽场所入口：<br>–描述屏蔽场所允许与该设备使用的其他设备的发射参数； <br>– 允许的设备清单；<br>– 禁止使用的设备类型清单。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>5.2.2.3</p></td>
    <td><p>Requirements applicable to ME EQUIPMENT that intentionally receives RF<br>electromagnetic energy for the purpose of its operation对有意接收射频信号的（出于功能目的）ME设备的要求</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT that<br>intentionally receives RF electromagnetic energy for the purpose of its operation (RF<br>receivers), the technical description shall include the following information:<br>– each frequency or frequency band of reception;<br>– the preferred frequency or frequency band, if applicable; and<br>– the bandwidth of the receiving section of the ME EQUIPMENT in those bands.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，对于出于功能目的而有意接收射频信号的ME设备，其技术说明中应包含以下信息：<br>– 每个接收频率或频率范围；<br>– 如果适用，优选的频率或频率范围；和<br>– ME设备接收部分在上述频段中带宽。</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>5.2.2.4</p></td>
    <td><p>Requirements applicable to ME EQUIPMENT that includes RF transmitters对含有射频发射机的ME设备的要求</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT that<br>includes RF transmitters, the technical description shall include each frequency or frequency<br>band of transmission, the type and frequency characteristics of the modulation and the<br>EFFECTIVE RADIATED POWER.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，含有射频发射机的ME设备，其技术说明中应包含所有的发射频率或频率范围，调制类型和频率特性以及有效辐射功率。</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>Requirements applicable to PERMANENTLY INSTALLED LARGE ME EQUIPMENT and<br>LARGE ME SYSTEMS 对永久性安装大型ME设备和大型ME设备的要求</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for PERMANENTLY INSTALLED<br>LARGE ME EQUIPMENT and LARGE ME SYSTEMS for which the exemption specified in 8.6 from thetesting requirements of IEC 61000-4-3 is used, the technical description shall include the<br>following information:作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，永久性安装大型ME设备和大型ME设备，如果采用了8.6中对IEC61000-4-3测试要求的豁免，其技术说明中应包含以下信息： </p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>a) a statement that an exemption has been used and that the equipment has not been tested<br>for radiated RF IMMUNITY over the entire frequency range 80 MHz to 6 000 MHz;声明：适用了豁免，且设备没有在 80 MHz 到 6 000 MHz 的全部频率范围内进行射频辐射骚扰测试；</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>b) a warning to the effect that “WARNING: This equipment has been tested for radiated RF<br>immunity only at selected frequencies, and use nearby of emitters at other frequencies<br>could result in improper operation”; and 警告：该设备在特定的频率范围内进行了射频辐射骚扰测试，设备附近适用的其它频段的发射器可能会导致设备不正确的运行。和</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>c) a list of the frequencies and modulations used to test the IMMUNITY of the ME EQUIPMENT or<br>ME SYSTEM.用于测试ME设备或ME系统抗扰性的频率和调制说明清单。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>

    <td><p>5.2.2.6</p></td>
    <td><p>Requirements applicable to ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS that claim<br>compatibility with HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT对声称与高频手术设备兼容的ME设备或ME系统的要求</p></td>

  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT and<br>ME SYSTEMS that claim compatibility with HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT, the technical description<br>shall include a statement of HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT compatibility and the conditions of<br>INTENDED USE during HF surgery.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，声称与高频手术设备兼容的ME设备或ME系统，其技术说明中应包含与高频手术设备兼容性的声明，且技术说明中应称述与高频手术设备的兼容性和在高频手术中预期的使用条件。</p></td>

    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.2 of the general standard, ME EQUIPMENT and ME SYSTEMS<br>specified for use only in a shielded location SPECIAL ENVIRONMENT shall be labelled with a<br>CLEARLY LEGIBLE warning that they should be used only in the specified type of shielded<br>location.作为对IEC 60601-1 中7.2的补充，仅用于屏蔽场所的ME设备或ME系统，应清楚标识：只能用于指定类型的屏蔽场所。</p></td>

    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.2 of the general standard, the instructions for use shall<br>include the following:作为对IEC 60601-1中7.9.2的补充，使用说明中应包含如下内容：</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>a statement of the environments for which the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM is suitable.<br>Relevant exclusions, as determined by RISK ANALYSIS, shall also be listed, e.g. hospitals<br>except for near active HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT and the RF shielded room of an ME SYSTEM<br>for magnetic resonance imaging, where the intensity of EM DISTURBANCES is high.a)对ME设备或ME系统适用的环境说明，应罗列通过风险分析确定的不能使用的环境，如：除了靠近有源高频手术设备的医院和用于核磁共振成像的具有高电磁干扰强度的RF屏蔽室。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>

    <td><p><strong>Notice</strong><br>13. Do not measure body temperature in an environment with strong EM interference (examples include places close to a working microwave, induction cooker, or cellphone in use) as EM interference may cause errors in the reading or even device failure.</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>b) * the performance of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM that was determined to be ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE and a description of what the OPERATOR can expect if the ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE is lost or degraded due to EM DISTURBANCES (the defined term “ESSENTIAL<br>PERFORMANCE” need not be used).ME设备或ME系统的基本性能以及，当由于电磁干扰使该关键性能缺失或下降时操作者可以进行的操作。（基本性能 “ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE”可以不体现在描述中）。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Electromagnetic compatibility information</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>c) * a warning statement to the effect that “WARNING: Use of this equipment adjacent to or<br>stacked with other equipment should be avoided because it could result in improper<br>operation. If such use is necessary, this equipment and the other equipment should be<br>observed to verify that they are operating normally.”<br>The MANUFACTURER of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM may provide a description or list of<br>equipment with which the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM has been tested in a stacked or<br>adjacent configuration and with which stacked or adjacent use resulted in normal<br>operation.c) 警告性声明：应避免此设备与其他设备连接,因为这可能导致设备的运行异常。如果这种使用是必须的，需要观察该设备及其他设备以确认他们是否能正常工作。ME设备或ME系统的制造商可以提供一份描述或设备清单，在此描述或清单中的设备已经完成与ME设备或ME系统的连接和测试且证明能正常工作。</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p><strong>Notice</strong><br>13. Do not measure body temperature in an environment with strong EM interference (examples include places close to a working microwave, induction cooker, or cellphone in use) as EM interference may cause errors in the reading or even device failure.</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>d) * a list of all cables and maximum lengths of cables (if applicable), transducers and other<br>ACCESSORIES that are replaceable by the RESPONSIBLE ORGANIZATION and that are likely to<br>affect compliance of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM with the requirements of Clause 7<br>(EMISSIONS) and Clause 8 (IMMUNITY). ACCESSORIES may be specified either generically<br>(e.g. shielded cable, load impedance) or specifically (e.g. by MANUFACTURER and MODEL OR<br>TYPE REFERENCE).<br>Transducers and cables specified by the MANUFACTURER of the ME EQUIPMENT or<br>ME SYSTEM as replacement parts for internal components need not be listed.应提供一份清单，清单应包含：所有的线缆以及线缆的最大长度（如果适用）、转换器和其它可被责任机构更换的且可能会影响ME设备或ME系统对本标准条款7和条款8的符合性的附件。可采用概括性语言（如屏蔽导线，负载电阻）或描述性的语言（如制造商或型号）来说明附件。制造商规定的，作为ME设备或ME系统可更换部件的内部转换器或内部线缆不用罗列。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>无线缆</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>e) * a warning statement to the effect that “WARNING: Use of accessories, transducers and<br>cables other than those specified or provided by the manufacturer of this equipment could<br>result in increased electromagnetic emissions or decreased electromagnetic immunity of<br>this equipment and result in improper operation.”e) 警告性声明：使用制造商提供或要求以外的附件、转换器和线缆可能会导致ME设备电磁辐射增加和抗电磁干扰能力下降，并且会导致不正常的运行。</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>

    <td><p>有USB线的适用</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>f) * a warning statement to the effect that: “WARNING: Portable RF communications<br>equipment (including peripherals such as antenna cables and external antennas) should<br>be used no closer than 30 cm (12 inches) to any part of the [ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM],<br>including cables specified by the manufacturer. Otherwise, degradation of the<br>performance of this equipment could result.”<br>In the above warning, “[ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM]” shall be replaced with the MODEL OR<br>TYPE REFERENCE of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM.f) 警告性声明：可携带式射频通讯设备（包括包括外围设备，如天线、电缆和外接天线等）在使用时，应与ME设备或ME系统的任一部件（包括制造商要求的线缆）保持至少30cm(12 inches)的距离，否则可能会导致该设备性能下降。<br>在上述警告中“ME设备或ME系统”应被设备的型号替代。</p></td>

    <td><p>If higher IMMUNITY TEST LEVELS than those specified in Table 9 are used, the minimum<br>separation distance may be lowered. Lower minimum separation distances shall be calculated<br>using the equation specified in 8.10.如果采用了比Table 9更高的抗扰测试等级,上述要求的最小距离可以降低。降低后的最小距离应按照8.10中罗列的公式计算。</p></td>

    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.2 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT and<br>ME SYSTEMS that are classified class A according to CISPR 11, the instructions for use shall<br>include the following note:<br>NOTE The EMISSIONS characteristics of this equipment make it suitable for use in industrial areas and hospitals<br>(CISPR 11 class A). If it is used in a residential environment (for which CISPR 11 class B is normally required) this<br>equipment might not offer adequate protection to radio-frequency communication services. The user might need to<br>take mitigation measures, such as relocating or re-orienting the equipment.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.2 的补充，CISPR 11中A类ME设备和ME系统，其使用说明中应包含以下备注：<br>备注：该设备的发射特性使其适合于工业环境和医院使用(CISPR 11 class A)。 如果在居住环境中使用该设备（此类设备需要符合CISPR 11 class B的要求），该设备可能无法对射频通讯服务提供足够的保护。使用者可能需要采取补救措施，如设备的搬迁或重新定向。</p></td>

    <td><p>c) * all necessary instructions for maintaining BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE with<br>regard to ELECTROMAGNETIC DISTURBANCES for the EXPECTED SERVICE LIFE.所有维持基本安全性能所需的与预期使用寿命内电磁干扰相关的介绍。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>d) one or more of the following and a recommendation that a notice containing this<br>information be posted at the entrance(s) to the shielded location:<br>– a specification of the EMISSIONS characteristics of other equipment allowed inside the<br>shielded location with the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM;<br>– a list of specific equipment allowed;<br>– a list of types of equipment prohibited.以下要求中的一条或多条，以及建议：包含此信息的通知应粘贴在在屏蔽场所入口：<br>–描述屏蔽场所允许与该设备使用的其他设备的发射参数； <br>– 允许的设备清单；<br>– 禁止使用的设备类型清单。</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT that<br>intentionally receives RF electromagnetic energy for the purpose of its operation (RF<br>receivers), the technical description shall include the following information:<br>– each frequency or frequency band of reception;<br>– the preferred frequency or frequency band, if applicable; and<br>– the bandwidth of the receiving section of the ME EQUIPMENT in those bands.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，对于出于功能目的而有意接收射频信号的ME设备，其技术说明中应包含以下信息：<br>– 每个接收频率或频率范围；<br>– 如果适用，优选的频率或频率范围；和<br>– ME设备接收部分在上述频段中带宽。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT that<br>includes RF transmitters, the technical description shall include each frequency or frequency<br>band of transmission, the type and frequency characteristics of the modulation and the<br>EFFECTIVE RADIATED POWER.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，含有射频发射机的ME设备，其技术说明中应包含所有的发射频率或频率范围，调制类型和频率特性以及有效辐射功率。</p></td>

    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>In addition to the requirements of 7.9.3 of the general standard, for ME EQUIPMENT and<br>ME SYSTEMS that claim compatibility with HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT, the technical description<br>shall include a statement of HF SURGICAL EQUIPMENT compatibility and the conditions of<br>INTENDED USE during HF surgery.作为对IEC 60601-1 7.9.3的补充，声称与高频手术设备兼容的ME设备或ME系统，其技术说明中应包含与高频手术设备兼容性的声明，且技术说明中应称述与高频手术设备的兼容性和在高频手术中预期的使用条件。</p></td>

    <td><p>4.2.2</p></td>
    <td><p>Environmental conditions of transport and storage between uses 使用的运输和存储条件</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>The instructions for use shall indicate the permissible environmental conditions of transport and storage of ME EQUIPMENT after the ME EQUIPMENT has been removed from its protective packaging and subsequently between uses. <br>使用说明书应说明ME设备在去掉保护性包装后，使用允许的运输</p></td>
    <td><p>A</p></td>
    <td><p>检查使用说明书</p></td>
    <td><p>OK</p></td>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Product performance </p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Unless otherwise indicated in the instructions for use or if the ME EQUIPMENT is STATIONARY, the ME EQUIPMENT shall comply with this standard and shall remain operational in NORMAL USE within its specification after transport or storage in the following environmental temperature range:<br>除非在说明书中另外规定或者ME设备是固定式的，ME设备应在以下的运输和存储之后，按本标准以及能按说明书正常操作</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>a) 依据使用说明书为ME 设备运输和存储准备<br>Prepare the ME EQUIPMENT for transportation or storage according to instructions for use.<br>b) 将ME 设备至于规定的最低运输和存储条件（温度： 0  -4℃）：<br>Expose the ME EQUIPMENT at its lowest specified environmental transport and storage conditions (temperature   0  -4°C) for:<br>——最少24 h；或at least 24 h; or<br>——确保设备达到热稳态最少2 h<br> ensure that the ME EQUIPMENT reaches THERMAL STABILITY for at least 2 h.<br>c) 将ME 设备至于规定的最高运输和存储条件（温度：+4  0℃，RH:±3%）<br>Then expose the ME EQUIPMENT at its highest specified environmental transport and storage conditions (temperature  +4  0°C and relative humidity ± 3 %) for:</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>————</p></td>
    <td><p>另有规定：<br>-25~55℃<br>≤95%</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>—— – 25 °C without relative humidity control; and<br>-25℃，无温湿度控制</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>——最少24 h；或at least 24 h; or
——确保设备达到热稳态最少2 h，由低温到高温的过度应当足够缓慢，以提供不结露的环境
ensure that the ME EQUIPMENT reaches THERMAL STABILITY for at least 2 h. 
The transition from low to high conditions should be made slowly enough to provide a noncondensing environment.</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>——</p></td>
    <td><p>同上</p></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>—— + 70 °C at a relative humidity up to 93 %, non-condensing;<br>+70℃，RH：93%不结露</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>d) 在此条件结束后，ME 设备返回并稳定在正常操作条件下
At the end of this conditioning period, allow the ME EQUIPMENT to return and stabilize at the operating conditions of NORMAL USE.
e)评价ME设备的性能，并确保其人提供基本安全和基本性能
Evaluate the ME EQUIPMENT to its specifications and ensure that it provides BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL ERFORMANCE.</p></td>

    <td><p>The instructions for use shall indicate the permissible environmental operating conditions of the ME EQUIPMENT.<br>使用说明书中应说明ME 设备的操作条件</p></td>

  <tr>
    <td><p></p></td>
    <td><p>Unless otherwise indicated in the instructions for use, the ME EQUIPMENT shall comply with its specifications and all the requirements of this standard when operated in NORMAL USE under the following environmental operating conditions:<br>除非使用说明书中另有规定，否则ME 设备应在以下条件下操作符合其规格以及其他要求<br>——   a temperature range of + 5 °C to + 40 °C;<br>温度范围：+5℃~40℃</p></td>
    <td><p>NA</p></td>
    <td><p>a) 将ME 设备至于环境（常态）条件：Expose the ME EQUIPMENT to the ambient conditions for
——最少6 h；或at least 6 h, or
——确保设备达到热稳态最少2 h ensure that the ME EQUIPMENT reaches THERMAL STABILITY for at least 2 h.
b) 评价ME 设备的性能规格并确定其提供BS和EP
Evaluate the ME EQUIPMENT to its specifications and ensure that it provides BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE.
c) 当在最低大气压力条件下，评价ME 设备的性能规格并确定其提供提供BS和EP
 Evaluate the ME EQUIPMENT to its specifications and ensure that it provides BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE while at the lowest specified atmospheric pressure.
d) 当在最高大气压力条件下，评价ME 设备的性能规格并确定其提供提供BS和EP
Evaluate the ME EQUIPMENT to its specifications and ensure that it provides BASIC SAFETY and ESSENTIAL PERFORMANCE while at the highest specified atmospheric pressure.
e) 冷却至最低环境操作条件，（温度： 0  -4℃，RH:≤15%）
Cool the ME EQUIPMENT to its lowest specified environmental operating conditions (temperature 0  -4 °C and relative humidity less than or equal to 15 %).</p></td>

    <td><p>– the ENCLOSURE of the ME EQUIPMENT shall be marked with its degree of protection and<br>safety sign ISO 7010-W001 (see IEC 60601-1:2005, Table D.2, safety sign 2),<br>• as well as with &#x27;keep dry&#x27;, or<br>• the symbol ISO 15223-1:2012, 5.3.4 (ISO 7000-0626 (2004-01)) (see Table C.1,<br>symbol 1).<br>ME设备外壳应标记它的防护等级和安全标识ISO 7010-W001(见IEC60601-1：2005，表D.2,安全标识2)<br>和“keep dry”,或<br>符号ISO15223-1:2012,5.3.4(ISO 7000-0626)(2004-01)) (见表C.1，符号1)<br><img src="images/469f956ceab6542b9034f50b70b6d4cac4d9e9b36b92fad574907e98ef0cd278.jpg"/></p></td>

This shall include:<br>随机文件应包括对非专业操作者及非专业组织机构介绍详细的必要的健康护理信息，已知的的任何禁忌以及预防措施，包括：<br>—— precautions to be taken in the event of changes in the performance of the ME EQUIPMENT or ME SYSTEM;<br>ME 设备性能改变的预防措施</p></td>

    <td><p>for ME EQUIPMENT, ME SYSTEMS, their parts or ACCESSORIES that are intended for other than single use and that can become contaminated through contact with the PATIENT or with body fluids or expired gases during INTENDED USE, the instructions for use shall:<br>ME 设备的部件及附件，预期非单独使用并可能通过患者连接或体液、呼吸造成污染，使用说明书应：<br>either,<br>—— indicate the frequency of cleaning, cleaning and disinfection or cleaning and sterilization, as appropriate, of the ME EQUIPMENT, ME SYSTEMS, parts or ACCESSORIES used on the same PATIENT including methods for rinsing, drying, handling and storage between uses (see 8.1 and 8.2); and<br>说明ME 设备在同一患者上使用的部件或附件清洗、消毒和灭菌的频次，冲洗、烘干、搬运、存储的方法；（8.1和8.2）和</p></td>

    <td rowspan="3"><p>5.10.1 Thermometer and Accessories:<br>体温计及附件</p></td>
    <td><p>5.10.1.1 A thermometer shall clearly indicate the units of its temperature scale.<br>温度计应清楚标明其温标单位。</p></td>
